(defprotocol mtls13 diffie-hellman

                  (cat
                    (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                      spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                    (enc
                      (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x))
                        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                        (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
                  (hash
                    (hash "fk"
                      (hash "sht" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
                        sr))
                    (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                      spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                    (enc
                      (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x))
                        (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                        (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))) c cpk
                (enc c cpk (privk ca))) (invk cpk)))
          (hash "cht" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))))
    ((recv
       (hash "cap" cr sr (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))
      (send
        (hash "cap" cr sr
          (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
    ((recv
       (cat "cap" cr sr (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))
      (send
        (cat "cap" cr sr
          (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))
    ((recv (cat "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))
      (send (cat "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))
    ((recv (cat "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))
      (send (cat "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))
    ((recv (cat (exp (gen) y) x)) (send (cat (exp (gen) y) x))))

  (parent 32)
  (unrealized (6 0))
  (dead)
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (cpk cpk) (s s) (c c) (ca ca) (x x)
        (y y) (request request) (response response))))

(defprotocol mtls13 diffie-hellman
  (defrole client
    (vars (s c ca name) (cr sr text) (spk cpk akey)
      (request response text) (x rndx) (y expt))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "fk"
                (hash "sht" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
              (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc s spk (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                  spk (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash "sht" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc (cat c cpk (enc c cpk (privk ca)))
          (enc
            (hash
              (cat
                (cat
                  (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
                    spk (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                  (enc
                    (hash (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
                      s spk (enc s spk (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
                (hash
                  (hash "fk"
                    (hash "sht" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr
                      sr))
                  (cat (cat cr (exp (gen) x)) (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s
